    function render() {
      const filtered = visibleAssets.filter(assetMatches);
      const sectionKeys = categorySelect.value === "all" ? typeOrder : [categorySelect.value];
      const sections = sectionKeys.map(typeKey => {
        const sectionAssets = categorySelect.value === "all"
          ? filtered.filter(asset => asset.primaryTypeKey === typeKey)
          : filtered.filter(asset => asset.typeKeys.includes(typeKey));
        if (!sectionAssets.length) return "";
        return `
          <section class="asset-section" aria-label="${typeNames[typeKey]}">
            <div class="category-header">
              <h3 class="category-title"><span class="dimension-mark">2D</span>${typeNames[typeKey]}</h3>
              <span class="tag">${sectionAssets.length} 个入口</span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid">${sectionAssets.map(renderCard).join("")}</div>
          </section>
        `;
      }).join("");
      assetGrid.innerHTML = sections;

      resultCount.textContent = `${filtered.length} 个结果`;
      emptyState.style.display = filtered.length ? "none" : "block";
      document.querySelectorAll(".style-button").forEach(button => {
        button.classList.toggle("active", button.dataset.style === styleSelect.value);
      });
    }

    function renderSources() {
      const visibleProviders = new Set(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider));

        <article class="source-card">
          <strong><a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">${name}</a></strong>
          <p>${note}</p>

    function render() {
      const filtered = visibleAssets.filter(assetMatches);
      const sectionKeys = categorySelect.value === "all" ? typeOrder : [categorySelect.value];
      const sections = sectionKeys.map(typeKey => {
        const sectionAssets = categorySelect.value === "all"
          ? filtered.filter(asset => asset.primaryTypeKey === typeKey)
          : filtered.filter(asset => asset.typeKeys.includes(typeKey));
        if (!sectionAssets.length) return "";
        return `
          <section class="asset-section" aria-label="${typeNames[typeKey]}">
            <div class="category-header">
              <h3 class="category-title"><span class="dimension-mark">2D</span>${typeNames[typeKey]}</h3>
              <span class="tag">${sectionAssets.length} 个入口</span>
            </div>
            <div class="grid">${sectionAssets.map(renderCard).join("")}</div>
          </section>
        `;
      }).join("");
      assetGrid.innerHTML = sections;

      resultCount.textContent = `${filtered.length} 个结果`;
      emptyState.style.display = filtered.length ? "none" : "block";
      document.querySelectorAll(".style-button").forEach(button => {
        button.classList.toggle("active", button.dataset.style === styleSelect.value);
      });
    }

    function renderSources() {
      const visibleProviders = new Set(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider));

        <article class="source-card">
          <strong><a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">${name}</a></strong>
          <p>${note}</p>

        ? `${visibleAssets.length} 个素材入口 / ${typeOrder.length} 个用途分类`
        : `${filtered.length} 个结果`;
      emptyState.style.display = filtered.length ? "none" : "block";
      document.querySelectorAll(".usage-button").forEach(button => {
        button.classList.toggle("active", button.dataset.type === selectedTypeKey);
      });
    }

    function renderSources() {
      const visibleProviders = new Set(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider));
      const visibleSources = sources.filter(([name]) => name === "Creative Commons" || visibleProviders.has(name));
      document.querySelector("#sourceGrid").innerHTML = visibleSources.map(([name, url, note]) => `
        <article class="source-card">
          <strong><a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">${name}</a></strong>
          <p>${note}</p>
        </article>
      `).join("");
    }

    document.querySelector("#statAssets").textContent = visibleAssets.length;
    document.querySelector("#statStyles").textContent = unique(visibleAssets.flatMap(asset => asset.typeKeys)).length;
    document.querySelector("#statSources").textContent = unique(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider)).length;

    renderUsageControls();
    renderSources();
    render();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

    function renderSources() {
      const visibleProviders = new Set(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider));
      const visibleSources = sources.filter(([name]) => name === "Creative Commons" || visibleProviders.has(name));
      document.querySelector("#sourceGrid").innerHTML = visibleSources.map(([name, url, note]) => `
        <article class="source-card">
          <strong><a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">${name}</a></strong>
          <p>${note}</p>
        </article>
      `).join("");
    }

    document.querySelector("#statAssets").textContent = visibleAssets.length;
    document.querySelector("#statStyles").textContent = unique(visibleAssets.flatMap(asset => asset.typeKeys)).length;
    document.querySelector("#statSources").textContent = unique(visibleAssets.map(asset => asset.provider)).length;

    renderUsageControls();
    renderSources();
    render();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
